Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in heat theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 461 and the invariant I(A,B) = 813 mod 5.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the heat space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 461 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 813. QED.

Theorem 219.4 (Invariance Principle)
